In this ping pong range just sell the high and buy the low until either one breaks and re-entry at retest... https://www.readthemarket.com/index.php/en/forum/journals/1534-luska 31/35 7/27/2018 Luska - RTM Community HOME EDUCATION FORUM MORE LOGOUT Your statement here is near perfect. When a pair is rangebound buying the lower demand levels and selling the higher supply levels targeting the next nearest fresh zone will allow a trader to increase their risk/reward ratio as well as increasing their potential to hit targets. If the range breaks, waiting on that retest is a high probabilty winner. Key point in all, let the market come to you and if you're willing to wait... it always comes knocking. I dont even know what the news or what the bullsh*t its talking about but i know its RED News a major one and something is gonna happen. As a technical trader, i trade solely on what the chart is telling me... Gonna need experience for it. Only God knows how hard i fell... See that a Marabuzo or railroad track watever you call it. This shows when the bulls are happily buying, someone ( you know who, the ones who controlled the market) wiped out those buyers. What happen is, this will result a psychology of the massive to induce sell. SO the bulls were either stopped out or holding to their losses. Then more sellers comes in. The mindset is now diverted to Selling.The people who trade against you doesnt want to lose out when they are selling and retails are selling too. so they need to bring in more sellers before they reverse the market. What happen is, they need to clear the leftover sell orders from that marabuzo. And they did. So no more sellers there. When the trend exhausted, they reverse the market drastically to wipe clean the open sell orders of the retailers who was a buyer and then change to a seller. These are the traders who trade the weather. They didnt know wat the hell is going on..More grey hairs will grow here. Next pic is how the return of the price to a level where the resistance change to support. I bet the retailers will place their stop loss just below that RBR. They know where you will put your stop loss. https://www.readthemarket.com/index.php/en/forum/journals/1534-luska?start=100 31/35 7/27/2018 Luska - Page 5 - RTM Community HOME EDUCATION FORUM MORE LOGOUT So how the hell they wanna profit with so many buyers in the market? Simple... NEWS!!!! if you were on ur station while the news is happening, u will see that the price drops like theres no tomorrow.... Now the buyers before was stopped out. The psychology is changed to selling again. More retailers chase the sell. But we SND traders will wait at the demand... But did u noticed that you will be stopped out by that spike and if u place a sellstop orders like some does will get triggered. Then it reversed... See how these bastards stop your buy orders and triggers your sell stops before they reverse... SHould be enough forr them to profit both ways... And the cycle continues again and again.... So remember, if you wanna do pending, make sure you have ample space for your stop loss. Always think the reversed way... although it might not work everytime as proven by my number of losses.It does helps you to profit more than your losses. Now you have to decide, either place your entry at the start of the base but place your stoploss slightly further to get filled OR Place your entry where the stoploss is supposed to be but you wont get your orders triggered most of the time but when it does, your reward is far more greater than you imagined. You dont need to learn so much about the chart but if you can spot the emotions on the chart, you will be trading just like these bastards and profit like them. Happy profiting Guys.. Very nice USD CAD trade, I was watching this pair, and thought it would react but from 1.0583 area not from 1.0568 !! Thanks allot Dont trade anything in those boxes... Look for trade from 1am to 4am NY time above or below the blue box which is the asian session which is also outside the box, this is the time they hunt stops. Evryone was taught to trade breakout. So since the massive are trading breakout, if i were the market, i would trigger their entries and reverse. Then the next trade for continuation or reversal happens from 7:30am to 11am NY time. the reason i use m15 is for entries and its my trading frame. SND bases i look in H1 and above. They wont trade the same direction for 3 consecutive sessions. Unless the higher tf permits a direction then you may hold the trade. Else just end the trade earlier before 5pm NY as and intraday trade. Do note that timing are just guidelines and not fixed, else the percentage of winners will be 95% instead of 5%. The market rewards the 95% 1 out of the 3 attempt in order for them to stay in the market. With such mindset when a breakout trader wins, will make him feel that his methods works and continue to do what he does. Thats why we are taught to trade 1 to 3% of our equity. Its all about emotions which is 60% of the time. Strategy is only 10% and money management is 30% .... If you can understand stop hunts on a daily basis, you will be able to capture 50 pips almost everyday and by compounding only 3% per trade, you can turn a $5k account to $225k in 50 trades or lets say 10 weeks.try to measure your daily candle from wick to wick and see if you are able to get 50 pips or more. So who says picking tops and bottom is impossible? You just need to learn how its being made and capture it. Mark your Yesterday high and yesterday low, weekly high and low, monthly high and low and asian session high and low everyday and u will be able to see how price reacts to these as stop hunts. https://www.readthemarket.com/index.php/en/forum/journals/1534-luska?start=150 6/37 7/27/2018 Luska - Page 7 - RTM Community HOME EDUCATION FORUM MORE LOGOUT The following user(s) said Thank You: hyoga, MozarTT QUICK REPLY REPLY QUOTE THANK YOU Luska #155 Luska Offline Trader Posts: 283 Thank you received: 313 Luska wrote: So far so good .. In this ping pong range just sell the high and buy the low until either one breaks and re-entry at retest... https://www.readthemarket.com/index.php/en/forum/journals/1534-luska 31/35 7/27/2018 Luska - RTM Community HOME EDUCATION FORUM MORE LOGOUT Your statement here is near perfect. When a pair is rangebound buying the lower demand levels and selling the higher supply levels targeting the next nearest fresh zone will allow a trader to increase their risk/reward ratio as well as increasing their potential to hit targets. If the range breaks, waiting on that retest is a high probabilty winner. Key point in all, let the market come to you and if you're willing to wait... it always comes knocking. I dont even know what the news or what the bullsh*t its talking about but i know its RED News a major one and something is gonna happen. As a technical trader, i trade solely on what the chart is telling me... Gonna need experience for it. Only God knows how hard i fell... See that a Marabuzo or railroad track watever you call it. This shows when the bulls are happily buying, someone ( you know who, the ones who controlled the market) wiped out those buyers. What happen is, this will result a psychology of the massive to induce sell. SO the bulls were either stopped out or holding to their losses. Then more sellers comes in. The mindset is now diverted to Selling.The people who trade against you doesnt want to lose out when they are selling and retails are selling too. so they need to bring in more sellers before they reverse the market. What happen is, they need to clear the leftover sell orders from that marabuzo. And they did. So no more sellers there. When the trend exhausted, they reverse the market drastically to wipe clean the open sell orders of the retailers who was a buyer and then change to a seller. These are the traders who trade the weather. They didnt know wat the hell is going on..More grey hairs will grow here. Next pic is how the return of the price to a level where the resistance change to support. I bet the retailers will place their stop loss just below that RBR. They know where you will put your stop loss. https://www.readthemarket.com/index.php/en/forum/journals/1534-luska?start=100 31/35 7/27/2018 Luska - Page 5 - RTM Community HOME EDUCATION FORUM MORE LOGOUT So how the hell they wanna profit with so many buyers in the market? Simple... NEWS!!!! if you were on ur station while the news is happening, u will see that the price drops like theres no tomorrow.... Now the buyers before was stopped out. The psychology is changed to selling again. More retailers chase the sell. But we SND traders will wait at the demand... But did u noticed that you will be stopped out by that spike and if u place a sellstop orders like some does will get triggered. Then it reversed... See how these bastards stop your buy orders and triggers your sell stops before they reverse... SHould be enough forr them to profit both ways... And the cycle continues again and again.... So remember, if you wanna do pending, make sure you have ample space for your stop loss. Always think the reversed way... although it might not work everytime as proven by my number of losses.It does helps you to profit more than your losses. Now you have to decide, either place your entry at the start of the base but place your stoploss slightly further to get filled OR Place your entry where the stoploss is supposed to be but you wont get your orders triggered most of the time but when it does, your reward is far more greater than you imagined. You dont need to learn so much about the chart but if you can spot the emotions on the chart, you will be trading just like these bastards and profit like them. Happy profiting Guys.. Very nice USD CAD trade, I was watching this pair, and thought it would react but from 1.0583 area not from 1.0568 !! Thanks allot Dont trade anything in those boxes... Look for trade from 1am to 4am NY time above or below the blue box which is the asian session which is also outside the box, this is the time they hunt stops. Evryone was taught to trade breakout. So since the massive are trading breakout, if i were the market, i would trigger their entries and reverse. Then the next trade for continuation or reversal happens from 7:30am to 11am NY time. the reason i use m15 is for entries and its my trading frame. SND bases i look in H1 and above. They wont trade the same direction for 3 consecutive sessions. Unless the higher tf permits a direction then you may hold the trade. Else just end the trade earlier before 5pm NY as and intraday trade. Do note that timing are just guidelines and not fixed, else the percentage of winners will be 95% instead of 5%. The market rewards the 95% 1 out of the 3 attempt in order for them to stay in the market. With such mindset when a breakout trader wins, will make him feel that his methods works and continue to do what he does. Thats why we are taught to trade 1 to 3% of our equity. Its all about emotions which is 60% of the time. Strategy is only 10% and money management is 30% .... If you can understand stop hunts on a daily basis, you will be able to capture 50 pips almost everyday and by compounding only 3% per trade, you can turn a $5k account to $225k in 50 trades or lets say 10 weeks.try to measure your daily candle from wick to wick and see if you are able to get 50 pips or more. So who says picking tops and bottom is impossible? You just need to learn how its being made and capture it. Mark your Yesterday high and yesterday low, weekly high and low, monthly high and low and asian session high and low everyday and u will be able to see how price reacts to these as stop hunts. https://www.readthemarket.com/index.php/en/forum/journals/1534-luska?start=150 6/37 7/27/2018 Luska - Page 7 - RTM Community HOME EDUCATION FORUM MORE LOGOUT The following user(s) said Thank You: hyoga, MozarTT QUICK REPLY REPLY QUOTE THANK YOU Luska #155 Luska Offline Trader Posts: 283 Thank you received: 313 Luska wrote: So far so good .. In this ping pong range just sell the high and buy the low until either one breaks and re-entry at retest... https://www.readthemarket.com/index.php/en/forum/journals/1534-luska 31/35 7/27/2018 Luska - RTM Community HOME EDUCATION FORUM MORE LOGOUT Your statement here is near perfect. When a pair is rangebound buying the lower demand levels and selling the higher supply levels targeting the next nearest fresh zone will allow a trader to increase their risk/reward ratio as well as increasing their potential to hit targets. If the range breaks, waiting on that retest is a high probabilty winner. Key point in all, let the market come to you and if you're willing to wait... it always comes knocking. I dont even know what the news or what the bullsh*t its talking about but i know its RED News a major one and something is gonna happen. As a technical trader, i trade solely on what the chart is telling me... Gonna need experience for it. Only God knows how hard i fell... See that a Marabuzo or railroad track watever you call it. This shows when the bulls are happily buying, someone ( you know who, the ones who controlled the market) wiped out those buyers. What happen is, this will result a psychology of the massive to induce sell. SO the bulls were either stopped out or holding to their losses. Then more sellers comes in. The mindset is now diverted to Selling.The people who trade against you doesnt want to lose out when they are selling and retails are selling too. so they need to bring in more sellers before they reverse the market. What happen is, they need to clear the leftover sell orders from that marabuzo. And they did. So no more sellers there. When the trend exhausted, they reverse the market drastically to wipe clean the open sell orders of the retailers who was a buyer and then change to a seller. These are the traders who trade the weather. They didnt know wat the hell is going on..More grey hairs will grow here. Next pic is how the return of the price to a level where the resistance change to support. I bet the retailers will place their stop loss just below that RBR. They know where you will put your stop loss. https://www.readthemarket.com/index.php/en/forum/journals/1534-luska?start=100 31/35 7/27/2018 Luska - Page 5 - RTM Community HOME EDUCATION FORUM MORE LOGOUT So how the hell they wanna profit with so many buyers in the market? Simple... NEWS!!!! if you were on ur station while the news is happening, u will see that the price drops like theres no tomorrow.... Now the buyers before was stopped out. The psychology is changed to selling again. More retailers chase the sell. But we SND traders will wait at the demand... But did u noticed that you will be stopped out by that spike and if u place a sellstop orders like some does will get triggered. Then it reversed... See how these bastards stop your buy orders and triggers your sell stops before they reverse... SHould be enough forr them to profit both ways... And the cycle continues again and again.... So remember, if you wanna do pending, make sure you have ample space for your stop loss. Always think the reversed way... although it might not work everytime as proven by my number of losses.It does helps you to profit more than your losses. Now you have to decide, either place your entry at the start of the base but place your stoploss slightly further to get filled OR Place your entry where the stoploss is supposed to be but you wont get your orders triggered most of the time but when it does, your reward is far more greater than you imagined. You dont need to learn so much about the chart but if you can spot the emotions on the chart, you will be trading just like these bastards and profit like them. Happy profiting Guys.. Very nice USD CAD trade, I was watching this pair, and thought it would react but from 1.0583 area not from 1.0568 !! Thanks allot Dont trade anything in those boxes... Look for trade from 1am to 4am NY time above or below the blue box which is the asian session which is also outside the box, this is the time they hunt stops. Evryone was taught to trade breakout. So since the massive are trading breakout, if i were the market, i would trigger their entries and reverse. Then the next trade for continuation or reversal happens from 7:30am to 11am NY time. the reason i use m15 is for entries and its my trading frame. SND bases i look in H1 and above. They wont trade the same direction for 3 consecutive sessions. Unless the higher tf permits a direction then you may hold the trade. Else just end the trade earlier before 5pm NY as and intraday trade. Do note that timing are just guidelines and not fixed, else the percentage of winners will be 95% instead of 5%. The market rewards the 95% 1 out of the 3 attempt in order for them to stay in the market. With such mindset when a breakout trader wins, will make him feel that his methods works and continue to do what he does. Thats why we are taught to trade 1 to 3% of our equity. Its all about emotions which is 60% of the time. Strategy is only 10% and money management is 30% .... If you can understand stop hunts on a daily basis, you will be able to capture 50 pips almost everyday and by compounding only 3% per trade, you can turn a $5k account to $225k in 50 trades or lets say 10 weeks.try to measure your daily candle from wick to wick and see if you are able to get 50 pips or more. So who says picking tops and bottom is impossible? You just need to learn how its being made and capture it. Mark your Yesterday high and yesterday low, weekly high and low, monthly high and low and asian session high and low everyday and u will be able to see how price reacts to these as stop hunts. https://www.readthemarket.com/index.php/en/forum/journals/1534-luska?start=150 6/37 7/27/2018 Luska - Page 7 - RTM Community HOME EDUCATION FORUM MORE LOGOUT The following user(s) said Thank You: hyoga, MozarTT QUICK REPLY REPLY QUOTE THANK YOU Luska #155 Luska Offline Trader Posts: 283 Thank you received: 313 Luska wrote: So far so good ..
